# Foldie Project — Parts Research (full detail)

Up to date 25 Oct 2026. 1984 listings, about 887 with an estimated weight or price (`est.`, basis shown). Prices in S$ at fixed rates (US$1=S$1.30, £1=S$1.70, €1=S$1.50), before shipping, GST and duty. Includes Bjorn Cycles, the BBInfinite and other T20-plan brands, the Brombacher shop (Korea) and Gemini / Sixweel. Carousell, Marketplace, Shopee and Lazada could not be read. The same data is in the Foldie Build page for the C Line, G Line / T20 and Aceoffix builds (fit notes tell which builds a row suits).

Compact list: `C_Line_Parts_List.md`.


---

1. **Bjorn Cycles Sedlo (full carbon, unpadded), each** — orig. US$330; where: Fair Wheel Bikes (US); maker ships from Slovenia; https://www.fairwheelbikes.com/bjorn-sedlo-carbon-saddle.html; fit: Lightest. Rider limit 120 kg. Hard shell, no padding. Needs 8-9 mm+ rail clamp
2. **Bjorn Cycles Probka, each** — orig. US$330; where: Fair Wheel Bikes (out of stock); used on Buycycle; https://www.fairwheelbikes.com/bjorn-probka-saddle.html; fit: Fits listed 143 or 155 mm. Used 155 mm ad US$236 = S$307 (listing, seen via search snippet)
3. **Bjorn Cycles Kort (short nose), each** — orig. US$440; where: Fair Wheel Bikes (out of stock); https://www.fairwheelbikes.com/bjorn-kort-saddle.html; fit: Wider front platform. 143/155 mm
4. **Bjorn Cycles Divan (padded), each** — orig. US$480; where: Fair Wheel Bikes (in stock); https://www.fairwheelbikes.com/bjorn-divan-saddle.html; fit: Padded, most comfortable. 143 mm listed
5. **Bjorn Cycles Setka (3D-printed padded), each** — orig. US$450; where: Fair Wheel Bikes (in stock); used Buycycle 143 mm; https://www.fairwheelbikes.com/bjorn-setka-saddle.html; fit: 143/155 mm. Most comfort per gram
